# Agents · T · part 9

988 entries, test-discoverer to test-impact-mapper. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-discoverer**: Read-only. Maps how a repo's tests must be written before any test is drafted — the runner and what it actually runs, the public surface, the outermost upstream seams, real in-process forms of dependencies, a precedent test worth copying, state and isolation constraints. Launched by the write-test skill; returns a cited map, never writes. ([ezzabuzaid/agent-plugin](https://github.com/ezzabuzaid/agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/coding/agents/test-discoverer.md) · ★4)
- **test-discoverer**: Use this agent to discover and implement additional critical Playwright tests beyond what the user specified. This agent analyzes the page for error states, missing coverage, and important features that should be tested. ([trusted-american/marketplace](https://github.com/trusted-american/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/playwright/agents/test-discoverer.md))
- **test-doc**: Use this agent when the user needs markdown files created in the test-files/ directory. ([lfr-ai/copier-fullstack-template](https://github.com/lfr-ai/copier-fullstack-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdd.md) · 2 copies)
- **test-driven-coder**: Use this agent when you have failing tests that need implementation code to make them pass. This agent follows strict TDD principles and writes the minimal code necessary to satisfy test requirements. ([Omcodes23/speedrun-arbitrum-vibekit](https://github.com/Omcodes23/speedrun-arbitrum-vibekit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-driven-coder.md))
- **test-driven-development**: Strict TDD implementation agent. Triggers on "implement", "write code for", "add feature", "fix bug", or any request that produces production Rust code. Always writes a failing test before touching implementation files. ([89jobrien/godmode](https://github.com/89jobrien/godmode/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/test-driven-development.md) · ★1)
- **test-driven-development**: Use when implementing a feature or bugfix, before writing the production code - write the failing test first, SEE it fail for the right reason with the narrow test command, write the minimum code to pass, then refactor; never commit red. Covers infra work (behaviour/acceptance table, source-level guard tests proven with a mutant), impossible fixtures and sandboxed tests. Also triggers on 'tdd', 'test first', 'write the failing test', 'red green refactor'. Runs as a subagent; the test-driven-development skill hands the work here. ([dxiiren/project-skeleton](https://github.com/dxiiren/project-skeleton/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-driven-development.md) · 2 copies)
